        <p dir="auto">Hi <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/eekhoorn01/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/eekhoorn01">@eekhoorn01</a>, thanks for the report. From the stack trace, I assume the following happens:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>You use Jacoco code coverage</li>
<li>Jacoco adds the <code class="notranslate">$jacocoInit(...)</code> method to <code class="notranslate">io.quarkiverse.cxf.QuarkusCxfContextUtils</code></li>
<li>Our class transformer that replaces method bodies of <code class="notranslate">org.apache.cxf.ws.addressing.ContextUtils</code> by calls to same-name methods of <code class="notranslate">io.quarkiverse.cxf.QuarkusCxfContextUtils</code> fails at checking that all methods of <code class="notranslate">io.quarkiverse.cxf.QuarkusCxfContextUtils</code> were replaced.</li>
<li>It's because there is no such method <code class="notranslate">$jacocoInit(...)</code> in <code class="notranslate">org.apache.cxf.ws.addressing.ContextUtils</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">That solve that for you, we could perhaps ignore Jacoco methods.</p>
<p dir="auto">Does that sound OK to you?</p>

        <p dir="auto">We could actually ignore all synthetic methods as we are quite sure that the methods to which want to delegate are non-synthetic. That would make it work also for other possible frameworks, which inject methods for whatever reasons.</p>
    </td>
  </tr>

    </tbody>
  </table>
</task-lists>

<p dir="auto">Is there something changed in the quarkiverse cxf library between version 3.33.3.3 and 3.33.4 concerning this issue?</p>

<p dir="auto">Yes, the code throwing the exception is new in Quarkus Platform 3.33.4 (which uses Quarkus CXF 3.33.12 under the hood).</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">It is correct that we use Jacoco code coverage.</p>
</blockquote>
